### [1] [ChatSVA: Bridging SVA Generation for Hardware Verification via Task-Specific LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02811)
*Lik Tung Fu,Jie Zhou,Shaokai Ren,Mengli Zhang,Jia Xiong,Hugo Jiang,Nan Guan,Xi Wang,Jun Yang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了ChatSVA，一个基于多智能体框架的端到端SystemVerilog断言生成系统，通过AgentBridge平台解决数据稀缺问题，在功能正确性上相比SOTA提升33.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能验证占据IC开发周期超50%，手动编写SystemVerilog断言（SVA）费时易错。大语言模型虽有潜力，但直接部署面临功能准确率低和领域数据严重稀缺的双重挑战。

Method: 提出了ChatSVA系统，建立在多智能体框架之上；核心为AgentBridge平台，能够系统性地生成高纯度数据集，从而克服少样本场景下的数据稀缺问题。

Result: 在24个RTL设计上评估，ChatSVA实现了98.66%的语法通过率和96.12%的功能通过率，每个设计平均生成139.5个SVA，功能覆盖率达82.50%。相比此前的SOTA，功能正确性提升了33.3个百分点，功能覆盖率提升超11倍。

Conclusion: ChatSVA不仅确立了自动化SVA生成的新SOTA，还为解决少样本、特定领域场景下的长链推理问题建立了稳健框架，并已公开发布在线服务。

Abstract: Functional verification consumes over 50% of the IC development lifecycle, where SystemVerilog Assertions (SVAs) are indispensable for formal property verification and enhanced simulation-based debugging. However, manual SVA authoring is labor-intensive and error-prone. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise, their direct deployment is hindered by low functional accuracy and a severe scarcity of domain-specific data. To address these challenges, we introduce ChatSVA, an end-to-end SVA generation system built upon a multi-agent framework. At its core, the AgentBridge platform enables this multi-agent approach by systematically generating high-purity datasets, overcoming the data scarcity inherent to few-shot scenarios. Evaluated on 24 RTL designs, ChatSVA achieves 98.66% syntax and 96.12% functional pass rates, generating 139.5 SVAs per design with 82.50% function coverage. This represents a 33.3 percentage point improvement in functional correctness and an over 11x enhancement in function coverage compared to the previous state-of-the-art (SOTA). ChatSVA not only sets a new SOTA in automated SVA generation but also establishes a robust framework for solving long-chain reasoning problems in few-shot, domain-specific scenarios. An online service has been publicly released at https://www.nctieda.com/CHATDV.html.

</details>


### [2] [EEspice: A Modular Circuit Simulation Platform with Parallel Device Model Evaluation via Graph Coloring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03079)
*Xuanhao Bao,Danial Chitnis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了EEspice，一个开源电路仿真框架，通过模块化架构和并行stamp策略，在64核工作站上实现了最高45倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代模拟/混合信号设计越来越依赖优化在环流程（如AI和基于LLM的尺寸代理），需要高效准确的仿真器。然而，BSIM模型等非线性模型的计算成本仍是瓶颈。传统并行方法中，晶体管等器件可跨处理器分配，但stamp阶段（将器件贡献添加到共享系统矩阵）因多核竞争更新相同矩阵元素而成为串行瓶颈。

Method: 提出EEspice开源框架，采用模块化架构将器件模型评估解耦为独立可替换的内核，实现并行stamp策略。核心方法是将MOSFET实例划分为独立的颜色组，使这些组可并行处理，避免矩阵更新冲突。

Result: 在64核工作站上，当冲突较少时，该方法相比单线程性能实现了高达45倍的加速。性能分析表明加速效果依赖于电路拓扑结构。

Conclusion: EEspice通过创新的并行stamp策略成功克服了传统电路仿真中的关键瓶颈，为现代IC设计提供了高效的基础设施，特别适合AI驱动的优化流程。

Abstract: As modern analogue/mixed-signal design increasingly relies on optimization-in-the-loop flows, such as AI and LLM-based sizing agents that repeatedly invoke SPICE-efficient, accurate high-performance simulators have become an indispensable foundation for modern integrated circuit (IC) design. However, the computational cost of evaluating nonlinear models, particularly for BSIM models, remains a significant bottleneck. In standard parallelization approaches, devices such as transistors are easily distributed across processors. The subsequent stamping phase, where each device's contributions are added to the shared system matrix, often creates a bottleneck. Because multiple processor cores compete to update the same matrix elements simultaneously, the system is forced to process tasks one at a time to avoid errors. This paper introduces EEspice, an open-source circuit simulation framework whose modular architecture decouples device model evaluation into independently replaceable kernels, enabling a parallel stamping strategy that overcomes this bottleneck. It partitions MOSFET instances into independent color groups, which can be processed in parallel. Our results show that on a 64-core workstation, the proposed approach achieves up to 45x speedup over single-thread performance when conflicts are low. Our analysis also explores how performance depends on circuit topology.

</details>


### [3] [InCoder-32B-Thinking: Industrial Code World Model for Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03144)
*Jian Yang,Wei Zhang,Jiajun Wu,Junhang Cheng,Tuney Zheng,Fanglin Xu,Weicheng Gu,Lin Jing,Yaxin Du,Joseph Li,Yizhi Li,Yan Xing,Chuan Hao,Ran Tao,Ruihao Gong,Aishan Liu,Zhoujun Li,Mingjie Tang,Chenghua Lin,Siheng Chen,Wayne Xin Zhao,Xianglong Liu,Ming Zhou,Bryan Dai,Weifeng Lv*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出InCoder-32B-Thinking模型，通过ECoT框架合成错误驱动的推理链，结合工业代码世界模型实现跨领域的代码生成与自验证，在通用和工业基准测试中达到开源最佳水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业软件开发（芯片设计、GPU优化、嵌入式系统）领域缺乏展示工程师如何推理硬件约束和时序语义的专家推理轨迹，现有的代码生成模型难以捕捉工业场景中关键的错误修正过程。

Method: 提出Error-driven Chain-of-Thought (ECoT)合成框架，通过与环境交互的多轮对话和错误反馈生成推理链；训练工业代码世界模型(ICWM)学习代码对硬件行为的因果影响，实现在编译前预测执行结果以进行自验证；所有合成的推理轨迹都通过领域工具链验证。

Result: InCoder-32B-Thinking在14个通用基准和9个工业基准上取得优异成绩：LiveCodeBench v5达到81.3%，CAD-Coder达到84.0%，KernelBench达到38.0%，在所有测试领域均达到开源模型的顶级水平。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模错误修正过程和学习工业代码的因果动态，该方法成功生成了符合工业任务自然推理深度分布的高质量训练数据，证明了将领域特定执行知识融入代码生成模型的有效性。

Abstract: Industrial software development across chip design, GPU optimization, and embedded systems lacks expert reasoning traces showing how engineers reason about hardware constraints and timing semantics. In this work, we propose InCoder-32B-Thinking, trained on the data from the Error-driven Chain-of-Thought (ECoT) synthesis framework with an industrial code world model (ICWM) to generate reasoning traces. Specifically, ECoT generates reasoning chains by synthesizing the thinking content from multi-turn dialogue with environmental error feedback, explicitly modeling the error-correction process. ICWM is trained on domain-specific execution traces from Verilog simulation, GPU profiling, etc., learns the causal dynamics of how code affects hardware behavior, and enables self-verification by predicting execution outcomes before actual compilation. All synthesized reasoning traces are validated through domain toolchains, creating training data matching the natural reasoning depth distribution of industrial tasks. Evaluation on 14 general (81.3% on LiveCodeBench v5) and 9 industrial benchmarks (84.0% in CAD-Coder and 38.0% on KernelBench) shows InCoder-32B-Thinking achieves top-tier open-source results across all domains.GPU Optimization

</details>

### [4] [High Volatility and Action Bias Distinguish LLMs from Humans in Group Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02578)
*Sahaj Singh Maini,Robert L. Goldstone,Zoran Tiganj*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究通过群体二分搜索博弈比较了LLM与人类的协调能力，发现LLM在缺乏直接沟通的群体协作任务中表现不佳，无法像人类那样适应并稳定策略，存在过度切换行为，且难以有效利用丰富反馈信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在群体协调方面表现出卓越的能力，随着大语言模型（LLM）能力的提升，一个开放性问题是：LLM是否能展现出与人类相当的适应性协调能力，以及它们是否使用与人类相同的策略。

Method: 研究者设计了一个n人参与的共同利益博弈——群体二分搜索（Group Binary Search），参与者在非完美监控条件下需要协调行动以达成共同目标。玩家独立提交数值以试图总和达到随机分配的目标数字，在没有直接沟通的情况下，依靠群体反馈迭代调整提交值直至达到目标。研究从反应性缩放、切换动态和跨博弈学习等机制层面指标进行分析。

Result: 研究发现，人类能够随时间推移适应并稳定其行为，而LLM往往无法在多次博弈中改进，且表现出过度切换行为，这损害了群体收敛。此外，更丰富的反馈（如数值误差幅度）对人类帮助显著，但对LLM的影响较小。

Conclusion: 通过基于人类基线和机制层面的分析指标，该研究揭示了人类与LLM群体在协调能力上的差异，为缩小协调差距提供了基于行为的诊断方法。

Abstract: Humans exhibit remarkable abilities to coordinate in groups. As large language models (LLMs) become more capable, it remains an open question whether they can demonstrate comparable adaptive coordination and whether they use the same strategies as humans. To investigate this, we compare LLM and human performance on a common-interest game with imperfect monitoring: Group Binary Search. In this n-player game, participants need to coordinate their actions to achieve a common objective. Players independently submit numerical values in an effort to collectively sum to a randomly assigned target number. Without direct communication, they rely on group feedback to iteratively adjust their submissions until they reach the target number. Our findings show that, unlike humans who adapt and stabilize their behavior over time, LLMs often fail to improve across games and exhibit excessive switching, which impairs group convergence. Moreover, richer feedback (e.g., numerical error magnitude) benefits humans substantially but has small effects on LLMs. Taken together, by grounding the analysis in human baselines and mechanism-level metrics, including reactivity scaling, switching dynamics, and learning across games, we point to differences in human and LLM groups and provide a behaviorally grounded diagnostic for closing the coordination gap.

</details>


### [5] [Do Agent Societies Develop Intellectual Elites? The Hidden Power Laws of Collective Cognition in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02674)
*Kavana Venkatesh,Jiaming Cui*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM多智能体系统的协调动态进行了大规模实证研究，发现了三条耦合规律，揭示了导致系统扩展收益递减的“整合瓶颈”机制，并提出了DTI方法有效改善了协调失败处的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统作为交互式智能体社会被广泛应用，但在规模化时往往出现收益递减或不稳定现象，其原因尚不明确。为了理解这一现象，作者旨在通过大规模实证研究揭示系统内部的协调动态规律。

Method: 研究引入了原子事件级表述，将推理重构为协调级联；通过分析跨任务、拓扑和规模的150万次交互数据，识别出协调动态的规律。基于此，提出了整合瓶颈机制，并开发了“赤字触发整合”（DTI）方法，在失衡时有选择地增加整合强度。

Result: 发现了三条耦合规律：协调遵循重尾级联分布；通过优先连接形成智力精英集中；随着系统规模增长，极端事件愈发频繁。这些效应由整合瓶颈机制耦合。DTI方法在协调失败处显著提升性能，且未抑制大规模推理过程。

Conclusion: 研究确立了集体认知的定量规律，识别出协调结构是理解和改进可扩展多智能体智能的一个基础且此前未被测量的维度，为解决规模化扩展中的协调问题提供了理论依据和方法支持。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed as interacting agent societies, yet scaling these systems often yields diminishing or unstable returns, the causes of which remain poorly understood. We present the first large-scale empirical study of coordination dynamics in LLM-based multi-agent systems, introducing an atomic event-level formulation that reconstructs reasoning as cascades of coordination. Analyzing over 1.5 Million interactions across tasks, topologies, and scales, we uncover three coupled laws: coordination follows heavy-tailed cascades, concentrates via preferential attachment into intellectual elites, and produces increasingly frequent extreme events as system size grows. We show that these effects are coupled through a single structural mechanism: an integration bottleneck, in which coordination expansion scales with system size while consolidation does not, producing large but weakly integrated reasoning processes. To test this mechanism, we introduce Deficit-Triggered Integration (DTI), which selectively increases integration under imbalance. DTI improves performance precisely where coordination fails, without suppressing large-scale reasoning. Together, our results establish quantitative laws of collective cognition and identify coordination structure as a fundamental, previously unmeasured axis for understanding and improving scalable multi-agent intelligence.

</details>


### [6] [Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning-based Joint Design of Low-Carbon P2P Market and Bidding Strategy in Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02728)
*Junhao Ren,Honglin Gao,Sijie Wang,Lan Zhao,Qiyu Kang,Aniq Ashan,Yajuan Sun,Gaoxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对微电网社区可再生能源利用难题，提出了一种基于多智能体强化学习的日内P2P交易框架，在保障微电网自主决策和逐利行为的同时，实现了社区社会福利和碳减排目标的双赢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源发电的不确定性以及实时市场的不稳定性限制了微电网社区对清洁能源的有效利用；现有的点对点（P2P）和微电网协调方法通常依赖集中式优化或限制性协调规则，难以在实际应用中落地。

Method: 提出一种日内P2P交易框架。具体而言，将微电网的决策过程建模为去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（DEC-POMDP），并利用多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架进行求解。该方法赋予各微电网高度决策自主权，同时引入新型市场出清机制进行宏观调控，激励微电网优先消纳本地可再生能源。

Result: 仿真结果表明，自利竞价策略与P2P市场设计的结合显著提高了可再生能源利用率，并减少了对外部高碳电力的依赖。该框架在局部自主性、自利追求以及社区层面的经济和环境效益改善之间实现了平衡集成。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功实现了局部自治、个体逐利与社区整体经济社会效益及环境效益之间的平衡，为微电网社区清洁能源的高效消纳提供了一种可行的去中心化解决方案。

Abstract: The challenges of the uncertainties in renewable energy generation and the instability of the real-time market limit the effective utilization of clean energy in microgrid communities. Existing peer-to-peer (P2P) and microgrid coordination approaches typically rely on certain centralized optimization or restrictive coordination rules which are difficult to be implemented in real-life applications. To address the challenge, we propose an intraday P2P trading framework that allows self-interested microgrids to pursue their economic benefits, while allowing the market operator to maximize the social welfare, namely the low carbon emission objective, of the entire community. Specifically, the decision-making processes of the microgrids are formulated as a Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (DEC-POMDP) and solved using a Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework. Such an approach grants each microgrid a high degree of decision-making autonomy, while a novel market clearing mechanism is introduced to provide macro-regulation, incentivizing microgrids to prioritize local renewable energy consumption and hence reduce carbon emissions. Simulation results demonstrate that the combination of the self-interested bidding strategy and the P2P market design helps significantly improve renewable energy utilization and reduce reliance on external electricity with high carbon-emissions. The framework achieves a balanced integration of local autonomy, self-interest pursuit, and improved community-level economic and environmental benefits.

</details>


### [7] [Fully Byzantine-Resilient Distributed Multi-Agent Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02791)
*Haejoon Lee,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对拜占庭边攻击的分布式Q学习算法，通过基于冗余的过滤机制利用二跳邻居信息验证消息，实现了在受损通信网络下所有智能体价值函数几乎必然收敛到最优值函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的弹性多智能体强化学习（MARL）方法通常只能保证几乎必然收敛到近似最优值函数，或者需要严格的假设条件才能收敛到最优解，导致智能体在拜占庭攻击下可能无法学习到最优策略。

Method: 提出了一种新型分布式Q学习算法，核心是设计了一个基于冗余的过滤机制。该机制利用二跳邻居信息来验证传入消息的有效性，同时保持双向信息流。此外，引入了新的拓扑收敛条件，提出了系统性的网络构建方法，并证明该条件可在多项式时间内验证。

Result: 证明了在提出的算法下，所有智能体的价值函数能够几乎必然收敛到最优价值函数。提出了可多项式时间验证的拓扑条件。仿真实验表明，该方法在拜占庭边攻击下能收敛到最优解，而先前的方法则无法收敛。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了受损通信网络下分布式MARL收敛到最优解的问题，通过理论分析和实验验证证明了所提算法和拓扑条件的有效性，优于现有的弹性MARL方法。

Abstract: We study Byzantine-resilient distributed multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), where agents must collaboratively learn optimal value functions over a compromised communication network. Existing resilient MARL approaches typically guarantee almost sure convergence only to near-optimal value functions, or require restrictive assumptions to ensure convergence to optimal solution. As a result, agents may fail to learn the optimal policies under these methods. To address this, we propose a novel distributed Q-learning algorithm, under which all agents' value functions converge almost surely to the optimal value functions despite Byzantine edge attacks. The key idea is a redundancy-based filtering mechanism that leverages two-hop neighbor information to validate incoming messages, while preserving bidirectional information flow. We then introduce a new topological condition for the convergence of our algorithm, present a systematic method to construct such networks, and prove that this condition can be verified in polynomial time. We validate our results through simulations, showing that our method converges to the optimal solutions, whereas prior methods fail under Byzantine edge attacks.

</details>

### [8] [MLFCIL: A Multi-Level Forgetting Mitigation Framework for Federated Class-Incremental Learning in LEO Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02356)
*Heng Zhang,Xiaohong Deng,Sijing Duan,Wu Ouyang,KM Mahfujul,Yiqin Deng,Zhigang Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对低轨卫星星座的联邦类增量学习场景，提出了MLFCIL框架，通过多层次遗忘缓解和双粒度协调策略，有效解决了数据异构性、灾难性遗忘和稳定性-可塑性平衡问题，在准确率和遗忘缓解方面显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星星座日益增长的星上计算需求面临着严格的内存和通信约束，新类别的持续出现给协作训练带来重大挑战。现有的联邦类增量学习方法在面对低轨卫星特有的三个挑战时表现不足：轨道动态引起的非独立同分布数据异构性、聚合过程中放大的灾难性遗忘，以及在有限资源下平衡稳定性与可塑性的需求。

Method: 提出MLFCIL多层次遗忘缓解框架，将灾难性遗忘分解为三个来源并在不同层次解决：1）类重加权损失减少局部偏差；2）结合特征重放和原型引导漂移补偿的知识蒸馏来保留跨任务知识；3）类感知聚合缓解联邦过程中的遗忘。此外，设计了双粒度协调策略，结合轮次级自适应损失平衡和步级梯度投影，进一步增强稳定性-可塑性权衡。

Result: 在NWPU-RESISC45数据集上的实验表明，MLFCIL在准确率和遗忘缓解两方面均显著优于基线方法，同时引入的资源开销极小。

Conclusion: MLFCIL框架成功解决了低轨卫星星座场景下联邦类增量学习的关键挑战，通过多层次遗忘缓解机制和双粒度协调策略实现了高效的知识保留与新知识学习的平衡，为资源受限的卫星边缘计算环境提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-Earth-orbit (LEO) satellite constellations are increasingly performing on-board computing. However, the continuous emergence of new classes under strict memory and communication constraints poses major challenges for collaborative training. Federated class-incremental learning (FCIL) enables distributed incremental learning without sharing raw data, but faces three LEO-specific challenges: non-independent and identically distributed data heterogeneity caused by orbital dynamics, amplified catastrophic forgetting during aggregation, and the need to balance stability and plasticity under limited resources. To tackle these challenges, we propose MLFCIL, a multi-level forgetting mitigation framework that decomposes catastrophic forgetting into three sources and addresses them at different levels: class-reweighted loss to reduce local bias, knowledge distillation with feature replay and prototype-guided drift compensation to preserve cross-task knowledge, and class-aware aggregation to mitigate forgetting during federation. In addition, we design a dual-granularity coordination strategy that combines round-level adaptive loss balancing with step-level gradient projection to further enhance the stability-plasticity trade-off. Experiments on the NWPU-RESISC45 dataset show that MLFCIL significantly outperforms baselines in both accuracy and forgetting mitigation, while introducing minimal resource overhead.

</details>


### [9] [Dynamic Mask Enhanced Intelligent Multi-UAV Deployment for Urban Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02358)
*Gaoxiang Cao,Wenke Yuan,Yunpeng Hou,Huasen He,Quan Zheng,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-SDAM算法，利用多无人机作为中继节点增强城市车载自组网(VANETs)的连通性，在提升连通性18.2%的同时降低66.6%的能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市车载自组织网络(VANETs)面临频繁的链路断开和子网分割等挑战，导致网络连接可靠性受阻，需要有效的解决方案来增强网络连通性。

Method: 动态部署多架无人机(UAV)作为通信中继以增强VANETs；提出一种新颖的基于分数的动态动作掩码增强QMIX算法(Q-SDAM)，该算法设计了分数动态动作掩码机制，引导UAV智能体探索大规模动作空间，加速学习过程并提升优化性能。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上验证了Q-SDAM的实用性；相比现有算法，Q-SDAM在连通性方面提升了18.2%，同时能耗降低了66.6%。

Conclusion: 所提出的Q-SDAM算法能够有效解决城市VANETs的连接问题，在最大化车辆连通性的同时最小化多无人机能耗，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Vehicular Ad Hoc Networks (VANETs) play a crucial role in realizing vehicle-road collaboration and intelligent transportation. However, urban VANETs often face challenges such as frequent link disconnections and subnet fragmentation, which hinder reliable connectivity. To address these issues, we dynamically deploy multiple Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) as communication relays to enhance VANET. A novel Score based Dynamic Action Mask enhanced QMIX algorithm (Q-SDAM) is proposed for multi-UAV deployment, which maximizes vehicle connectivity while minimizing multi-UAV energy consumption. Specifically, we design a score-based dynamic action mask mechanism to guide UAV agents in exploring large action spaces, accelerate the learning process and enhance optimization performance. The practicality of Q-SDAM is validated using real-world datasets. We show that Q-SDAM improves connectivity by 18.2% while reducing energy consumption by 66.6% compared with existing algorithms.

</details>


### [10] [Fighting AI with AI: AI-Agent Augmented DNS Blocking of LLM Services during Student Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02360)
*Yonas Kassa,James Bonacci,Ping Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI-Sinkhole框架，用于在监考考试期间动态发现、语义分类并临时阻断新兴LLM聊天机器人服务，以维护学术评估的公正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在教育中的变革潜力（如提升可及性和个性化学习）正被其引发的学术评估挑战所掩盖。LLM可能导致学生绕过批判性思维、增加认知卸载，从而破坏学术严谨性。因此，需要在利用AI教育优势的同时，保护批判性思维和学术诚信。

Method: 提出了AI-Sinkhole框架，这是一个基于DNS的AI代理增强系统。该框架利用量化LLM（LLama 3、DeepSeek-R1、Qwen-3）作为可解释分类器，结合Pi-Hole实现动态DNS阻断，能够动态发现、语义分类并临时全网阻断新兴LLM聊天机器人服务。

Result: 使用LLM作为可解释分类器取得了稳健的跨语言性能，F1分数超过0.83。研究团队发布了初始代码和可直接部署的AI-Sinkhole阻断列表，以支持该领域的未来研究和开发。

Conclusion: AI-Sinkhole框架为教育机构提供了一种有效手段，在监考环境中动态识别和阻断LLM服务，有助于平衡AI的教育效益与学术诚信保护，为构建健康的AI教育生态系统提供了技术解决方案。

Abstract: The transformative potential of large language models (LLMs) in education, such as improving accessibility and personalized learning, is being eclipsed by significant challenges. These challenges stem from concerns that LLMs undermine academic assessment by enabling bypassing of critical thinking, leading to increased cognitive offloading. This emerging trend stresses the dual imperative of harnessing AI's educational benefits while safeguarding critical thinking and academic rigor in the evolving AI ecosystem. To this end, we introduce AI-Sinkhole, an AI-agent augmented DNS-based framework that dynamically discovers, semantically classifies, and temporarily network-wide blocks emerging LLM chatbot services during proctored exams. AI-Sinkhole offers explainable classification via quantized LLMs (LLama 3, DeepSeek-R1, Qwen-3) and dynamic DNS blocking with Pi-Hole. We also share our observations in using LLMs as explainable classifiers which achieved robust cross-lingual performance (F1-score > 0.83). To support future research and development in this domain initial codes with a readily deployable 'AI-Sinkhole' blockist is available on https://github.com/AIMLEdu/ai-sinkhole.

</details>


### [11] [TRACE: Traceroute-based Internet Route change Analysis with Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02361)
*Raul Suzuki,Rodrigo Moreira,Pedro Henrique A. Damaso de Melo,Larissa F. Rodrigues Moreira,Flávio de Oliveira Silva*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TRACE，一个仅使用traceroute延迟数据检测互联网路由变化的机器学习流水线，无需控制平面信息，通过特征工程和堆叠集成模型在F1分数上显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测互联网路由不稳定性是一项关键但充满挑战的任务，尤其是在仅依赖端点主动测量时，现有方法难以有效识别路由变化。

Method: 提出了一个名为TRACE的机器学习流水线，使用滚动统计和聚合上下文模式进行特征工程，并采用由超参数优化的元学习器精炼的梯度提升决策树堆叠集成架构，通过校准决策阈值解决类别不平衡问题。

Result: TRACE在F1分数上显著优于传统基线模型，在检测互联网路由变化方面表现出强大的有效性。

Conclusion: TRACE成功实现了仅依赖traceroute延迟数据检测路由变化的目标，证明了在不依赖控制平面信息的情况下也能有效识别路由不稳定性。

Abstract: Detecting Internet routing instability is a critical yet challenging task, particularly when relying solely on endpoint active measurements. This study introduces TRACE, a MachineLearning (ML)pipeline designed to identify route changes using only traceroute latency data, thereby ensuring independence from control plane information. We propose a robust feature engineering strategy that captures temporal dynamics using rolling statistics and aggregated context patterns. The architecture leverages a stacked ensemble of Gradient Boosted Decision Trees refined by a hyperparameter-optimized meta-learner. By strictly calibrating decision thresholds to address the inherent class imbalance of rare routing events, TRACE achieves a superior F1-score performance, significantly outperforming traditional baseline models and demonstrating strong effective ness in detecting routing changes on the Internet.

</details>


### [12] [Evaluating Small Language Models for Front-Door Routing: A Harmonized Benchmark and Synthetic-Traffic Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02367)
*Warren Johnson,Charles Lee*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 研究发现小型语言模型（SLM, 1-4B参数）已具备在亚秒级、零边际成本条件下完成模型路由分类任务的能力。在两项研究中，Qwen-2.5-3B在自托管模型中实现了帕累托最优（准确率0.793，中位延迟988ms），但所有模型均未达到生产就绪标准（准确率≥0.85且P95延迟≤2000ms）。SLM路由的成本和延迟条件已满足，但6-8个百分点的准确率差距及分类准确性对下游输出质量的影响尚待验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型路由方法依赖基于LLM的分类器或偏好训练的路由器，这些方法本身成本高昂且延迟较高，将多目标优化问题简化为单维质量预测。作者提出小型语言模型（SLM, 1-4B参数）现已具备足够的推理能力，可实现亚秒级、零边际成本、自托管的任务分类，使路由决策在推理预算中可忽略不计。

Method: 研究通过两项实验验证论点：（1）研究1为离线基准测试，在相同的Azure T4硬件、服务栈、量化和60案例语料库上，对比Phi-3.5-mini、Qwen2.5-1.5B和Qwen-2.5-3B在六标签分类任务上的表现；（2）研究2为预注册的四臂随机实验，在合成流量下（每臂60个有效样本）比较Phi-4-mini、Qwen-2.5-3B和DeepSeek-V3与无路由对照，同时评估准确率和P95延迟门控。

Result: 研究1中，Qwen-2.5-3B达到最高准确率（0.783），最佳延迟-准确率权衡，且是唯一在所有六类任务上均有非零准确率的模型。研究2中，DeepSeek-V3准确率最高（0.830）但未通过预注册的P95延迟门控（2295ms）；Qwen-2.5-3B在自托管模型中帕累托占优（准确率0.793，中位延迟988ms，零边际成本）。然而，没有模型满足独立可行性标准（准确率≥0.85，P95延迟≤2000ms）。

Conclusion: 基于SLM的路由已满足成本和延迟先决条件，但距离生产可行性仍有6-8个百分点的准确率差距。此外，正确的分类是否能转化为下游输出质量的提升尚未经测试，这是实现生产级部署剩余的关键挑战。

Abstract: Selecting the appropriate model at inference time -- the routing problem -- requires jointly optimizing output quality, cost, latency, and governance constraints. Existing approaches delegate this decision to LLM-based classifiers or preference-trained routers that are themselves costly and high-latency, reducing a multi-objective optimization to single-dimensional quality prediction. We argue that small language models (SLMs, 1-4B parameters) have now achieved sufficient reasoning capability for sub-second, zero-marginal-cost, self-hosted task classification, potentially making the routing decision negligible in the inference budget. We test this thesis on a six-label taxonomy through two studies. Study 1 is a harmonized offline benchmark of Phi-3.5-mini, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and Qwen-2.5-3B on identical Azure T4 hardware, serving stack, quantization, and a fixed 60-case corpus. Qwen-2.5-3B achieves the best exact-match accuracy (0.783), the strongest latency-accuracy tradeoff, and the only nonzero accuracy on all six task families. Study 2 is a pre-registered four-arm randomized experiment under synthetic traffic with an effective sample size of 60 unique cases per arm, comparing Phi-4-mini, Qwen-2.5-3B, and DeepSeek-V3 against a no-routing control. DeepSeek-V3 attains the highest accuracy (0.830) but fails the pre-registered P95 latency gate (2,295 ms); Qwen-2.5-3B is Pareto-dominant among self-hosted models (0.793 accuracy, 988 ms median, $0 marginal cost). No model meets the standalone viability criterion (>=0.85 accuracy, <=2,000 ms P95). The cost and latency prerequisites for SLM-based routing are met; the accuracy gap of 6-8 percentage points and the untested question of whether correct classification translates to downstream output quality bound the remaining distance to production viability.

</details>


### [13] [Beyond Message Passing: Toward Semantically Aligned Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02369)
*Dun Yuan,Fuyuan Lyu,Ye Yuan,Weixu Zhang,Bowei He,Jiayi Geng,Linfeng Du,Zipeng Sun,Yankai Chen,Changjiang Han,Jikun Kang,Alex Chen,Haolun Wu,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个受人类交流启发的三层框架（通信层、语法层、语义层），系统分析了18种代表性智能体通信协议，发现当前协议设计存在明显失衡：对传输、流式处理、模式定义和生命周期管理的支持日益成熟，但在澄清、上下文对齐和验证等语义层面的机制支持严重不足，导致语义责任被推给提示词或应用层，增加了互操作和维护成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）系统对工具使用、智能体协作和跨异构环境操作的需求增加，智能体通信协议正成为关键基础设施。然而，当前缺乏对该领域的系统性分析和框架化理解，尤其是协议在语义层面的支持能力尚不明确，这促使作者对现有协议进行全面梳理和评估。

Method: 提出一个受人类交流启发的三层分析框架（通信层、语法层、语义层），在此框架下系统分析18种代表性智能体通信协议，对比它们在可靠传输、结构化交互和语义级协调方面的支持能力，并识别当前协议生态中的技术债务形式。

Result: 分析发现当前协议设计存在明显失衡：大多数协议在传输、流式处理、模式定义和生命周期管理方面提供了日益成熟的支持，但在协议层面提供的澄清、上下文对齐和验证机制非常有限。语义责任常被推给提示词、包装器或特定应用的编排逻辑，造成了隐藏的互操作性和维护成本。

Conclusion: 识别了当前协议生态中的主要技术债务形式，提炼了在不同部署场景下选择协议的实用指导，并提出了迈向可互操作、安全且语义鲁棒的智能体生态系统的未来研究议程，强调应超越简单的消息传递，向共享理解方向发展。

Abstract: Agent communication protocols are becoming critical infrastructure for large language model (LLM) systems that must use tools, coordinate with other agents, and operate across heterogeneous environments. This work presents a human-inspired perspective on this emerging landscape by organizing agent communication into three layers: communication, syntactic, and semantic. Under this framework, we systematically analyze 18 representative protocols and compare how they support reliable transport, structured interaction, and meaning-level coordination. Our analysis shows a clear imbalance in current protocol design. Most protocols provide increasingly mature support for transport, streaming, schema definition, and lifecycle management, but offer limited protocol-level mechanisms for clarification, context alignment, and verification. As a result, semantic responsibilities are often pushed into prompts, wrappers, or application-specific orchestration logic, creating hidden interoperability and maintenance costs. To make this gap actionable, we further identify major forms of technical debt in today's protocol ecosystem and distill practical guidance for selecting protocols under different deployment settings. We conclude by outlining a research agenda for interoperable, secure, and semantically robust agent ecosystems that move beyond message passing toward shared understanding.

</details>


### [14] [Agentic AI-Empowered Wireless Agent Networks With Semantic-Aware Collaboration via ILAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02381)
*Zhouxiang Zhao,Jiaxiang Wang,Zhaohui Yang,Kun Yang,Zhaoyang Zhang,Mingzhe Chen,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出无线智能体网络(WAN)框架，通过渐进式知识聚合机制实现语义感知协作，显著提升能量效率和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体人工智能的快速发展推动无线网络向学习与通信集成(ILAC)范式演进，但实现高效智能体协作面临两大挑战：语义冗余处理困难，以及缺乏通信、计算、控制的集成机制。

Method: 提出无线智能体网络(WAN)框架，将聚合过程建模为联合能量最小化问题：智能体执行语义压缩消除冗余、优化传输功率传递语义载荷、调整物理轨迹主动增强信道质量。开发分层算法集成内层资源优化与外层拓扑演化，在拓扑演化中引入势场克服贪婪匹配的短视性。

Result: 理论分析证明在拓扑演化中引入势场能有效克服贪婪匹配的短视性，为长期能量最小化提供数学上严谨的启发式方法。仿真结果表明该框架相比传统基准实现更优的能量效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提框架在动态环境中实现了高效的语义感知协作，验证了语义感知协作在动态环境中的有效性，为未来无线网络从被动数据管道向智能协作生态系统演进提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The rapid development of agentic artificial intelligence (AI) is driving future wireless networks to evolve from passive data pipes into intelligent collaborative ecosystems under the emerging paradigm of integrated learning and communication (ILAC). However, realizing efficient agentic collaboration faces challenges not only in handling semantic redundancy but also in the lack of an integrated mechanism for communication, computation, and control. To address this, we propose a wireless agent network (WAN) framework that orchestrates a progressive knowledge aggregation mechanism. Specifically, we formulate the aggregation process as a joint energy minimization problem where the agents perform semantic compression to eliminate redundancy, optimize transmission power to deliver semantic payloads, and adjust physical trajectories to proactively enhance channel qualities. To solve this problem, we develop a hierarchical algorithm that integrates inner-level resource optimization with outer-level topology evolution. Theoretically, we reveal that incorporating a potential field into the topology evolution effectively overcomes the short-sightedness of greedy matching, providing a mathematically rigorous heuristic for long-term energy minimization. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves superior energy efficiency and scalability compared to conventional benchmarks, validating the efficacy of semantic-aware collaboration in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [15] [A Survey on AI for 6G: Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02370)
*Constantina Chatzieleftheriou,Eirini Liotou*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述了人工智能在6G网络中的关键作用，分析了深度学习、强化学习、联邦学习等核心技术，探讨了AI与网络功能的集成及其在URLLC、eMBB等服务领域的应用，并指出了可扩展性、安全性和能效等挑战及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无线通信技术的演进，人工智能（AI）在塑造第六代（6G）网络未来方面变得至关重要。结合AI与机器学习（ML），6G旨在为智慧城市、自主系统、全息临场和触觉互联网等应用提供高速率、低延迟和广泛的连接能力。因此，有必要全面梳理AI在支持6G网络中的角色与价值。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法，重点分析了深度学习、强化学习、联邦学习和可解释AI等关键技术，并探讨了这些技术与6G核心网络功能的集成方式。同时，针对可扩展性、安全性和能效等挑战提出了相应的解决方案。

Result: 研究总结了AI驱动的分析在6G服务领域的应用视角，涵盖超可靠低延迟通信（URLLC）、增强移动宽带（eMBB）、大规模机器类通信（mMTC）和通感一体化（ISAC）。同时，论文回应了关于标准化、伦理和可持续性方面的关切，并归纳了近期的研究趋势。

Conclusion: 本文为AI与下一代无线通信交叉领域的研究人员和从业者提供了有价值的参考，明确了AI在6G网络发展中的核心地位，并为未来的研究指明了方向。

Abstract: As wireless communication evolves, each generation of networks brings new technologies that change how we connect and interact. Artificial Intelligence (AI) is becoming crucial in shaping the future of sixth-generation (6G) networks. By combining AI and Machine Learning (ML), 6G aims to offer high data rates, low latency, and extensive connectivity for applications including smart cities, autonomous systems, holographic telepresence, and the tactile internet. This paper provides a detailed overview of the role of AI in supporting 6G networks. It focuses on key technologies like deep learning, reinforcement learning, federated learning, and explainable AI. It also looks at how AI integrates with essential network functions and discusses challenges related to scalability, security, and energy efficiency, along with new solutions. Additionally, this work highlights perspectives that connect AI-driven analytics to 6G service domains like Ultra-Reliable Low-Latency Communication (URLLC), Enhanced Mobile Broadband (eMBB), Massive Machine-Type Communication (mMTC), and Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC). It addresses concerns about standardization, ethics, and sustainability. By summarizing recent research trends and identifying future directions, this survey offers a valuable reference for researchers and practitioners at the intersection of AI and next-generation wireless communication.

</details>


### [16] [Cardinality is Not Enough: Super Host Detection via Segmented Cardinality Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02379)
*Yilin Zhao,Jiawei Huang,Xianshi Su,Weihe Li,Xin Li,Yan Liu,Jiacheng Xie,Qichen Su,Jin Ye,Wanchun Jiang,Jianxin Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出SegSketch，一种基于分段哈希的轻量级流基数估计方法，通过推断IP公共前缀长度实现子网级检测，在有限内存下显著提升超级主机检测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于sketch的超级主机检测方法依据完整IP地址估计流基数，忽略了恶意或受害超级主机通常与同一子网内主机通信的特性，导致高误报率和低准确性；而基于层级结构的方法虽能捕获子网内流基数，但存在内存开销过高的问题。

Method: 提出SegSketch，采用轻量级的折半分段哈希策略来推断IP地址的公共前缀长度，并在子网内进行基数估计，从而在受限内存条件下增强检测准确性。

Result: 在真实网络数据驱动下的实验表明，SegSketch的F1分数相比最先进解决方案提升高达8.04倍，尤其在较小内存预算下表现突出。

Conclusion: SegSketch有效解决了现有方法在准确性、误报率和内存开销之间的权衡问题，为资源受限环境下的超级主机检测提供了一种高效、准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurately detecting super host that establishes connections to a large number of distinct peers is significant for mitigating web attacks and ensuring high quality of web service. Existing sketch-based approaches estimate the number of distinct connections called flow cardinality according to full IP addresses, while ignoring the fact that a malicious or victim super host often communicates with hosts within the same subnet, resulting in high false positive rates and low accuracy. Though hierarchical-structure based approaches could capture flow cardinality in subnet, they inherently suffer from high memory usage. To address these limitations, we propose SegSketch, a segmented cardinality estimation approach that employs a lightweight halved-segment hashing strategy to infer common prefix lengths of IP addresses, and estimates cardinality within subnet to enhance detection accuracy under constrained memory size. Experiments driven by real-world traces demonstrate that, SegSketch improves F1-Score by up to 8.04x compared to state-of-the-art solutions, particularly under small memory budgets.

</details>


### [17] [RL-Loop: Reinforcement Learning-Driven Real-Time 5G Slice Control for Connected and Autonomous Mobility Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02461)
*Lara Tarkh,Ali Chouman,Hanan Lutfiyya,Abdallah Shami*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了RL-Loop框架，这是一个基于近端策略优化（PPO）的闭环强化学习系统，用于5G网络切片环境下的实时CPU资源控制；实验结果表明，该框架在保持服务质量可比的前提下，能够将平均CPU分配减少55%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智慧互联出行系统依赖5G边缘基础设施来支持实时通信、控制和服务差异化。然而，由于交通状况瞬息万变，现有的资源管理机制难以快速适应，因此迫切需要一种能够实时响应快速变化条件并自适应管理CPU资源的机制。

Method: 提出了一种名为RL-Loop的闭环强化学习框架。该框架部署了一个近端策略优化（PPO）智能体，持续观测切片级关键性能指标（KPIs），并在真实测试床上以一秒为粒度动态调整边缘CPU分配。该方法利用实时可观测性和反馈机制实现了自适应的、软件定义的边缘智能。

Result: 实验结果显示，与参考运行点相比，RL-Loop能够将平均CPU分配减少超过55%，同时达到可比的服务质量（QoS）劣化区域，证明了基于轻量级强化学习的反馈控制在资源管理上的高效性。

Conclusion: 研究证明了基于轻量级强化学习的反馈控制机制能够为支持智慧出行和互联车辆服务的5G边缘基础设施提供高效且响应迅速的资源管理方案，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Smart and connected mobility systems rely on 5G edge infrastructure to support real-time communication, control, and service differentiation. Achieving this requires adaptive resource management mechanisms that can react to rapidly changing traffic conditions. In this paper, we propose RL-Loop, a closed-loop reinforcement learning framework for real-time CPU resource control in 5G network slicing environments supporting connected mobility services. RL-Loop employs a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) agent that continuously observes slice-level key performance indicators and adjusts edge CPU allocations at one-second granularity on a real testbed. The framework leverages real-time observability and feedback to enable adaptive, software-defined edge intelligence. Experimental results suggest that RL-Loop can reduce average CPU allocation by over 55% relative to the reference operating point while reaching a comparable quality-of-service degradation region. These results indicate that lightweight reinforcement learning--based feedback control can provide efficient and responsive resource management for 5G-enabled smart mobility and connected vehicle services.

</details>


### [18] [Causal Inference for Quantifying Noisy Neighbor Effects in Multi-Tenant Cloud Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03145)
*Philipe S. Schiavo,João P. S. Milanezi,Moisés R. N. Ribeiro,Víctor M. G. Martínez,João Henrique Corrêa,José Marcos Nogueira,Fernando Frota Redigolo,Tereza C. Carvalho,Flávio de Oliveira Silva*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合受控实验与多阶段因果推断的分析方法，用于量化多租户云环境中的“吵闹邻居”问题严重性并建立租户间的因果关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多租户云环境中的资源共享虽然提高了成本效益，但引入了“吵闹邻居”问题，即共存的工作负载会不可预测地相互降低性能。尽管已有大量关于检测此类影响的研究，但目前缺乏可解释的方法来量化影响的严重程度并在租户之间建立因果关系。

Method: 提出一种分析方法，将受控实验与多阶段因果推断相结合，并在Kubernetes测试平台上进行了10轮独立验证。通过Granger因果分析统计性地建立因果关系，并为每个资源争用向量（CPU、内存、磁盘、网络）识别独特的“降级特征”。

Result: 该方法不仅量化了严重的性能降级（例如，在组合压力下I/O密集型工作负载性能下降高达67%），而且通过Granger因果分析统计性地建立了因果关系，发现当吵闹邻居激活时因果链增加了75%。此外，还识别了每种资源争用向量的独特“降级特征”，实现了超越异常检测的诊断能力。

Conclusion: 这项工作将“吵闹邻居”从一个难以捉摸的问题转变为可量化、可诊断的现象，为云运营商提供了可操作的见解，用于SLA管理和智能资源分配。

Abstract: Resource sharing in multi-tenant cloud environments enables cost efficiency but introduces the Noisy Neighbor problem, i.e., co-located workloads that unpredictably degrade each other's performance. Despite extensive research on detecting such effects, there are no explainable methodologies for quantifying the severity of impact and establishing causal relationships among tenants. We propose an analytical that combines controlled experimentation with multi-stage causal inference and validates it across 10 independent rounds in a Kubernetes testbed. Our methodology not only quantifies severe performance degradations (e.g., up to 67\% in I/O-bound workloads under combined stress) but also statistically establishes causality through Granger causality analysis, revealing a 75\% increase in causal links when the noisy neighbor activates. Furthermore, we identify unique "degradation signatures" for each resource contention vector (i.e., CPU, memory, disk, network), enabling diagnostic capabilities that go beyond anomaly detection. This work transforms the Noisy Neighbor from an elusive problem into a quantifiable, diagnosable phenomenon, providing cloud operators with actionable insights for SLA management and smart resource allocation.

</details>

### [19] [Convolutional Surrogate for 3D Discrete Fracture-Matrix Tensor Upscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02335)
*Martin Špetlík,Jan Březina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于3D卷积神经网络的代理模型，用于快速预测三维裂隙结晶介质中的等效水力传导张量，实现了100倍以上的计算加速，同时保持了高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维裂隙结晶介质中的地下水流动建模需要考虑由裂隙引起的强空间非均质性。细尺度离散裂隙-基质（DFM）模拟能够捕捉这种复杂性，但计算成本昂贵，特别是在需要重复评估时（如蒙特卡洛模拟）。传统的三维数值均匀化方法同样计算开销大，亟需开发高效准确的替代方法。

Method: 开发了一种代理模型，结合3D卷积神经网络和前馈层，用于从体素化的三维域预测等效水力传导张量Keq。该模型能够捕捉局部空间特征和全局交互作用。使用DFM模拟生成的数据训练了三个代理模型，分别对应不同的裂隙-基质传导率对比度。采用多层蒙特卡洛（MLMC）框架，在精度层级间过渡时使用数值均匀化来升级次分辨率裂隙效应。

Result: 训练后的模型在大多数测试案例中实现了高精度，归一化均方根误差低于0.22。在两个宏观尺度问题中验证了实用性：计算等效传导率张量和预测约束三维域的出流量。在两种情况下，基于代理模型的升级方法在保持精度的同时大幅降低了计算成本，在GPU上进行推理时实现了超过100倍的加速。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了一种高效准确的代理模型，能够替代传统的三维数值均匀化方法，显著降低了计算成本。该方法在多层蒙特卡洛框架下具有实际应用价值，为三维裂隙结晶介质中的地下水流动建模提供了一种实用的解决方案，特别适用于需要大量重复评估的场景。

Abstract: Modeling groundwater flow in three-dimensional fractured crystalline media requires accounting for strong spatial heterogeneity induced by fractures. Fine-scale discrete fracture-matrix (DFM) simulations can capture this complexity but are computationally expensive, especially when repeated evaluations are needed. To address this, we aim to employ a multilevel Monte Carlo (MLMC) framework in which numerical homogenization is used to upscale sub-resolution fracture effects when transitioning between accuracy levels.
  To reduce the cost of conventional 3D numerical homogenization, we develop a surrogate model that predicts the equivalent hydraulic conductivity tensor Keq from a voxelized 3D domain representing tensor-valued random fields of matrix and fracture conductivities. Fracture size, orientation, and aperture are sampled from distributions informed by natural observations.
  The surrogate architecture combines a 3D convolutional neural network with feed-forward layers, enabling it to capture both local spatial features and global interactions. Three surrogates are trained on data generated by DFM simulations, each corresponding to a different fracture-to-matrix conductivity contrast. Performance is evaluated across a wide range of fracture network parameters and matrix-field correlation lengths.
  The trained models achieve high accuracy, with normalized root-mean-square errors below 0.22 across most test cases. Practical applicability is demonstrated by comparing numerically homogenized conductivities with surrogate predictions in two macro-scale problems: computing equivalent conductivity tensors and predicting outflow from a constrained 3D domain. In both cases, surrogate-based upscaling preserves accuracy while substantially reducing computational cost, achieving speedups exceeding 100x when inference is performed on a GPU.

</details>


### [20] [Fast NF4 Dequantization Kernels for Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02556)
*Xiangbo Qi,Chaoyi Jiang,Murali Annavaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的共享内存优化技术，用于加速NVIDIA GPU上的NF4量化大语言模型推理，在保持生态兼容性的前提下实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型已超出单个GPU的内存容量，NF4量化虽能实现4倍内存压缩，但在当前NVIDIA GPU上推理时需要昂贵的反量化操作（转回FP16格式），这成为性能瓶颈，亟需优化方案。

Method: 提出一种轻量级的共享内存优化技术，通过合理的内存层次结构利用来解决问题。该方法利用共享内存相比全局内存访问具有12-15倍的延迟优势，通过简化索引逻辑减少指令计数，每个线程块仅使用64字节的共享内存。

Result: 与开源BitsAndBytes实现相比，在Gemma 27B、Qwen3 32B和Llama3.3 70B三个模型上实现了2.0-2.2倍的内核加速，端到端性能提升最高达1.54倍。

Conclusion: 轻量级优化可以以最小的工程投入带来实质性的性能提升。本研究为HuggingFace生态系统提供了即插即用的解决方案，使得在现有GPU基础设施上部署先进模型更加普及化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have grown beyond the memory capacity of single GPU devices, necessitating quantization techniques for practical deployment. While NF4 (4-bit NormalFloat) quantization enables 4$\times$ memory reduction, inference on current NVIDIA GPUs (e.g., Ampere A100) requires expensive dequantization back to FP16 format, creating a critical performance bottleneck. This paper presents a lightweight shared memory optimization that addresses this gap through principled memory hierarchy exploitation while maintaining full ecosystem compatibility. We compare our technique against the open-source BitsAndBytes implementation, achieving 2.0--2.2$\times$ kernel speedup across three models (Gemma 27B, Qwen3 32B, and Llama3.3 70B) and up to 1.54$\times$ end-to-end improvement by leveraging the 12--15$\times$ latency advantage of shared memory over global memory access. Our optimization reduces instruction counts through simplified indexing logic while using only 64 bytes of shared memory per thread block, demonstrating that lightweight optimizations can deliver substantial performance gains with minimal engineering effort. This work provides a plug-and-play solution for the HuggingFace ecosystem that democratizes access to advanced models on existing GPU infrastructure.

</details>


### [21] [Generating Counterfactual Patient Timelines from Real-World Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02337)
*Yu Akagi,Tomohisa Seki,Toru Takiguchi,Hiromasa Ito,Yoshimasa Kawazoe,Kazuhiko Ohe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于大规模真实世界数据训练的自回归生成模型能够生成临床合理的反事实轨迹，为个性化医疗和计算机模拟试验奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实模拟在个性化医疗和计算机模拟试验中具有变革性应用潜力，但现有方法存在局限性，难以有效实施。

Method: 开发了一种自回归生成模型，使用超过30万名患者和4亿条患者时间线条目的真实世界数据进行自监督训练，通过修改患者特征（如年龄、C反应蛋白、血清肌酐）来模拟替代临床场景下的患者轨迹。

Result: 在2023年COVID-19住院患者验证中，模型成功生成了临床合理的反事实轨迹：高龄、CRP升高和血清肌酐升高与住院死亡率增加相关；CRP升高时瑞德西韦处方增加，肾功能受损时瑞德西韦处方减少。这些结果重现了已知的临床模式。

Conclusion: 自回归生成模型能够基于真实世界数据有效建立反事实临床模拟的基础，为临床决策支持提供了新的方法论路径。

Abstract: Counterfactual simulation - exploring hypothetical consequences under alternative clinical scenarios - holds promise for transformative applications such as personalized medicine and in silico trials. However, it remains challenging due to methodological limitations. Here, we show that an autoregressive generative model trained on real-world data from over 300,000 patients and 400 million patient timeline entries can generate clinically plausible counterfactual trajectories. As a validation task, we applied the model to patients hospitalized with COVID-19 in 2023, modifying age, serum C-reactive protein (CRP), and serum creatinine to simulate 7-day outcomes. Increased in-hospital mortality was observed in counterfactual simulations with older age, elevated CRP, and elevated serum creatinine. Remdesivir prescriptions increased in simulations with higher CRP values and decreased in those with impaired kidney function. These counterfactual trajectories reproduced known clinical patterns. These findings suggest that autoregressive generative models trained on real-world data in a self-supervised manner can establish a foundation for counterfactual clinical simulation.

</details>


### [22] [AXELRAM: Quantize Once, Never Dequantize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02638)
*Yasushi Nishida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AXELRAM智能SRAM架构，直接从量化KV cache索引计算注意力分数，无需反量化。通过正交变换量化实现设计时固定码本，减少102.4倍查询乘法。发现并解决了符号模式敏感性导致的性能崩塌问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV cache量化方法需要在读取时进行反量化操作，引入额外的计算开销。研究动机在于设计一种能够直接在量化域进行注意力计算的硬件架构，消除反量化需求，从而大幅提升能效。

Method: 1) 采用正交变换量化，使每个坐标分布集中到N(0,1/d)，量化器仅依赖于维度d和位宽b，实现设计时固定码本；
2) 设计非对称路径：写入时变换，读取时查表且无需逆变换；
3) 发现符号模式敏感性导致性能崩塌，提出无梯度符号模式选择方法（200候选×8校准样本，一次性）。

Result: 1) 每查询乘法操作减少102.4倍；
2) 多种子评估发现：Qwen2.5-3B出现严重PPL崩塌(Delta > 50)，LLaMA-3.1-8B完全稳定；
3) 将SpinQuant的旋转方差现象从权重量化扩展到KV cache域，发现该效应更为严重；
4) 追踪根因至层间范数异构性。

Conclusion: AXELRAM成功实现了量化域内直接注意力计算，通过固定码本和符号模式选择技术，在零额外硬件成本下消除了灾难性性能崩塌，为高效KV cache处理提供了新的硬件-算法协同设计范式。

Abstract: We propose AXELRAM, a smart SRAM macro architecture that computes attention scores directly from quantized KV cache indices without dequantization. The key enabler is a design-time fixed codebook: orthogonal-transform-based quantization concentrates each coordinate's distribution to N(0,1/d), so the optimal quantizer depends only on dimension d and bit-width b, not on input data. The asymmetric path design -- transform on write, table-lookup on read with no inverse transform -- reduces per-query multiplications by 102.4x (a mathematical identity).
  Through multi-seed evaluation (10 seeds x 3 models), we discover that sign pattern sensitivity causes catastrophic PPL spikes (Delta > 50) on certain models (Qwen2.5-3B), while others (LLaMA-3.1-8B) are fully stable. This phenomenon extends SpinQuant's observation of rotation variance in weight quantization to the KV cache domain, where the effect is qualitatively more severe. We trace the root cause to layer-wise norm heterogeneity and propose a gradient-free sign pattern selection (200 candidates, 8 calibration samples, one-time) that eliminates catastrophic spikes with zero additional hardware cost. All source code is available at https://github.com/Axelidea/AXELRAM.

</details>


### [23] [LiME: Lightweight Mixture of Experts for Efficient Multimodal Multi-task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02338)
*Md Kowsher,Haris Mansoor,Nusrat Jahan Prottasha,Ozlem Garibay,Victor Zhu,Zhengping Ji,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LiME框架，通过轻量级调制替代适配器复制，在多任务适应中大幅降低可训练参数（最高减少4倍）并加快训练速度（最高29%），同时保持竞争力性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE-PEFT方法在多任务适应中，每个专家需要单独的适配器，导致可训练参数随专家数量线性增长，且仅适用于基于适配器的架构，限制了其可扩展性和通用性。

Method: 提出LiME框架：(1) 使用单一共享PEFT模块，通过轻量级专家向量调制其输出，替代适配器复制；(2) 引入零参数路由机制，利用现有冻结和适应表示消除每层的学习路由参数；(3) 结合n-gram窗口路由和基于路由置信度的自适应专家选择（Auto Top-K）。理论证明更多专家能保留更多任务相关信息，且调制能以有界误差逼近完整专家特定PEFT。

Result: 在包含47个任务（涵盖文本、图像、视频）的多模态多任务基准MMT-47上，LiME相比MoE-PEFT基线最高减少4倍可训练参数，训练速度提升最高29%，同时达到竞争性或更优的性能表现。

Conclusion: LiME成功解决了MoE-PEFT方法的参数效率瓶颈，通过轻量级调制机制实现了参数高效的多专家混合，具有良好的理论保证和实证效果，为多任务适应提供了更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: MoE-PEFT methods combine Mixture of Experts with parameter-efficient fine-tuning for multi-task adaptation, but require separate adapters per expert causing trainable parameters to scale linearly with expert count and limiting applicability to adapter-based architectures. We propose LiME (Lightweight Mixture of Experts), which achieves expert specialization through lightweight modulation rather than adapter replication. Instead of separate adapters, LiME uses a single shared PEFT module and modulates its output with lightweight expert vectors, reducing expert parameters while generalizing to any PEFT method. Notably, LiME introduces zero-parameter routing by leveraging existing frozen and adapted representations eliminating learned router parameters typically required per layer. Theoretically, we prove that (i) more experts preserve more task-relevant information and (ii) modulation approximates full expert-specific PEFT with bounded error. LiME further incorporates n-gram windowed routing and adaptive expert selection (Auto Top-K) based on routing confidence. Experiments on MMT-47, a multimodal multi-task benchmark with 47 tasks spanning text, image, and video, demonstrate that LiME achieves competitive or superior performance while using up to 4x fewer trainable parameters and up to 29% faster training compared to corresponding MoE-PEFT baselines.

</details>


### [24] [SIEVE: Sample-Efficient Parametric Learning from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02339)
*Parth Asawa,Alexandros G. Dimakis,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SIEVE方法，仅需3个查询示例即可实现样本高效的参数化学习，通过分解上下文生成高质量合成数据，将自然语言上下文内化为模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言上下文（指令、知识、反馈）包含丰富的适应信号。现有的上下文学习通过提示适应，而参数化学习虽能进一步提升性能但数据需求量大，严重依赖高质量轨迹或自动验证器。

Method: 提出SIEVE方法，包含新颖的合成数据生成流程SIEVE-GEN。核心思想是上下文可分解性：将上下文分解后，将合成查询仅与适用上下文配对生成更高质量的数据展开，再通过上下文蒸馏将上下文内化到模型权重中。

Result: 在需要上下文的推理任务中评估（包括自定义领域、RuleArena和Machine Translation from One Book任务），SIEVE仅用三个查询示例就超越了先前的上下文蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: SIEVE展示了如何从自然语言实现样本高效的参数化学习，证明了上下文分解策略能有效提升参数化学习的样本效率。

Abstract: Natural language context-such as instructions, knowledge, or feedback-contains rich signal for adapting language models. While in-context learning provides adaptation via the prompt, parametric learning persists into model weights and can improve performance further, though is data hungry and heavily relies on either high-quality traces or automated verifiers. We propose SIEVE, a method for sample-efficient parametric learning from natural language context that requires as few as three query examples. SIEVE uses a novel synthetic data generation pipeline, SIEVE-GEN, that leverages the insight that context is decomposable. Decomposing context allows us to generate higher quality rollouts by pairing synthetic queries with only the applicable context rather than the entirety, then using context distillation to internalize context into the model. We evaluate in reasoning settings where context is necessary, including custom domains and the RuleArena and Machine Translation from One Book tasks. Our results show that SIEVE outperforms prior context distillation methods using just three query examples, demonstrating how to achieve sample-efficient parametric learning from natural language.

</details>


### [25] [Not All Denoising Steps Are Equal: Model Scheduling for Faster Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02340)
*Ivan Sedykh,Nikita Sorokin,Valentin Malykh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出模型调度策略，在掩码扩散语言模型的去噪过程中动态替换小型模型，实现在保持生成质量的同时降低约17%的计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型虽然在生成质量上逐渐逼近自回归模型，但其采样过程计算开销巨大，需要多次完整的序列去噪过程且无法利用KV缓存加速，限制了其实际应用效率。

Method: 利用扩散框架的灵活性，研究模型调度策略：在部分去噪步骤中使用较小的MDLM替代完整的大模型。通过基于损失和KL散度的步骤重要性分析以及粗粒度步骤段的穷举搜索，识别出去噪轨迹中对模型替换最敏感的阶段。

Result: 在OpenWebText上的实验表明，去噪过程的早期和晚期步骤对模型替换具有较强鲁棒性，而中间步骤最为敏感。基于此发现，可在仅导致生成困惑度轻微下降的情况下，实现高达17%的FLOPs减少。

Conclusion: 简单且与架构无关的调度规则可以显著加速MDLM采样过程，同时基本保持以生成困惑度衡量的生成质量，为扩散语言模型的高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) narrow the quality gap to autoregressive LMs, but their sampling remains expensive because generation requires many full-sequence denoising passes with a large Transformer and, unlike autoregressive decoding, cannot benefit from KV caching. In this work, we exploit the flexibility of the diffusion framework and study model scheduling, where a smaller MDLM replaces the full model at a subset of denoising steps. On OpenWebText, we show that early and late denoising steps are substantially more robust to such replacement than middle steps, enabling up to a 17% reduction in FLOPs with only modest degradation in generative perplexity. We support these findings with a step-importance analysis based on loss and KL divergence between small and large models across timesteps, as well as an exhaustive search over coarse step segments, both of which identify the middle of the diffusion trajectory as most sensitive. Our results suggest that simple, architecture-agnostic scheduling rules can significantly accelerate MDLM sampling while largely preserving generation quality as measured by generative perplexity.

</details>


### [26] [LLM Reasoning with Process Rewards for Outcome-Guided Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02341)
*Mohammad Rezaei,Jens Lehmann,Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PROGRS框架通过结果条件居中方法处理过程奖励，在保持结果正确性主导的同时有效利用PRM，在多个数学推理基准上显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习流水线仅优化结果正确性，导致长链推理的反馈稀疏；而过程奖励模型(PRM)虽然提供密集监督，但其评分常与最终正确性不完全对齐，可能奖励局部流畅但最终错误的推理路径，绝对奖励优化会放大这种"流畅失败模式"并导致奖励黑客。

Method: 提出PROGRS框架，将过程奖励视为结果组内的相对偏好而非绝对目标。引入结果条件居中技术，在每个提示组内将错误轨迹的PRM分数平移至零均值，消除系统性偏差同时保留信息性排序。结合冻结的分位数回归PRM与多尺度一致性评估器，将居中后的过程奖励整合至GRPO中，无需辅助目标或额外可训练组件。

Result: 在MATH-500、AMC、AIME、MinervaMath和OlympiadBench基准上，PROGRS相比仅使用结果的基线方法一致提升了Pass@1性能，并以更少的rollout次数实现了更强的表现。

Conclusion: 结果条件居中方法能够安全有效地利用过程奖励进行数学推理，在保持结果正确性主导地位的同时，有效避免了PRM不完美对齐带来的奖励黑客问题。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning in large language models has improved substantially with reinforcement learning using verifiable rewards, where final answers can be checked automatically and converted into reliable training signals. Most such pipelines optimize outcome correctness only, which yields sparse feedback for long, multi-step solutions and offers limited guidance on intermediate reasoning errors. Recent work therefore introduces process reward models (PRMs) to score intermediate steps and provide denser supervision. In practice, PRM scores are often imperfectly aligned with final correctness and can reward locally fluent reasoning that still ends in an incorrect answer. When optimized as absolute rewards, such signals can amplify fluent failure modes and induce reward hacking.
  We propose PROGRS, a framework that leverages PRMs while keeping outcome correctness dominant. PROGRS treats process rewards as relative preferences within outcome groups rather than absolute targets. We introduce outcome-conditioned centering, which shifts PRM scores of incorrect trajectories to have zero mean within each prompt group. It removes systematic bias while preserving informative rankings. PROGRS combines a frozen quantile-regression PRM with a multi-scale coherence evaluator. We integrate the resulting centered process bonus into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) without auxiliary objectives or additional trainable components. Across MATH-500, AMC, AIME, MinervaMath, and OlympiadBench, PROGRS consistently improves Pass@1 over outcome-only baselines and achieves stronger performance with fewer rollouts. These results show that outcome-conditioned centering enables safe and effective use of process rewards for mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [27] [Characterizing WebGPU Dispatch Overhead for LLM Inference Across Four GPU Vendors, Three Backends, and Three Browsers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02344)
*Jędrzej Maczan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统量化了WebGPU在LLM推理（batch=1）中的分发开销，揭示了真实开销比单操作基准测试低约20倍，并开发了torch-webgpu后端，实现了CUDA性能的11-12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebGPU的安全设计引入了逐操作验证开销，在神经网络推理（特别是LLM batch=1场景）中由于大量小规模分发操作而累积，但该开销的真实成本缺乏系统性表征与研究。

Method: 提出了一种顺序分发方法论来准确测量开销，跨越4家GPU厂商、2个原生实现、3个浏览器、2个模型规模和3个操作系统进行系统性实验；并构建了基于PrivateUse1的torch-webgpu后端和FX-to-WebGPU编译器。

Result: WebGPU纯API开销为24-71μs（Vulkan 24-36μs，Metal 32-71μs），含Python的总开销约95μs；Vulkan上算子融合提升53%吞吐量；torch-webgpu达到CUDA性能的11-12%；RTX PRO 2000在float32下吞吐量为RTX 5090上WebGPU的1.4倍；后端选择是分发开销的主导因素。

Conclusion: 在batch=1场景下，当前分发密集型流水线中，无论算子质量如何，逐操作开销都是性能瓶颈；优化方向应聚焦于减少分发开销和算子融合，而非单纯提升算子计算效率。

Abstract: WebGPU's security-focused design imposes per-operation validation that compounds across the many small dispatches in neural network inference, yet the true cost of this overhead is poorly characterized. We present a systematic characterization of WebGPU dispatch overhead for LLM inference at batch size 1, spanning four GPU vendors (NVIDIA, AMD, Apple, Intel), two native implementations (Dawn, wgpu-native) and three browsers (Chrome, Safari, Firefox), and two model sizes (Qwen2.5-0.5B and 1.5B). Our primary contribution is a sequential-dispatch methodology that reveals naive single-operation benchmarks overestimate dispatch cost by ${\sim}20\times$. The true per-dispatch cost of WebGPU API overhead alone is 24-36 $μ$s on Vulkan and 32-71 $μ$s on Metal, while the total per-operation overhead including Python cost is ${\sim}95$~$μ$s, which turns out to be a distinction critical for optimization. On Vulkan, kernel fusion improves throughput by 53%, while CUDA fusion provides no benefit, confirming that per-operation overhead is a primary differentiator. LLM inference was tested across three major operating systems (Linux, Windows, macOS). We built $\texttt{torch-webgpu}$, a PrivateUse1-based out-of-tree PyTorch backend and an FX-to-WebGPU compiler, which on our reference platform achieves 11--12% of CUDA performance. At dtype-matched float32, RTX PRO 2000 achieves 1.4$\times$ WebGPU's throughput despite ${\sim}6\times$ less compute than RTX 5090. For dispatch overhead, backend choice is the dominant factor, although implementation choice also matters substantially within a backend (2.2$\times$ for Metal). In terms of dispatch vs kernel compute efficiency, we conclude that at batch=1 with the current dispatch-heavy pipeline, per-operation overhead dominates regardless of kernel quality. All code, benchmarks, and raw data are open source.

</details>


### [28] [Homophily-aware Supervised Contrastive Counterfactual Augmented Fair Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02342)
*Mahdi Tavassoli Kejani,Fadi Dornaika,Charlotte Laclau,Jean-Michel Loubes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种改进的反事实增强公平图神经网络模型，通过两阶段训练策略联合提升图神经网络的预测性能与公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络(GNNs)虽然在节点分类、链接预测等任务中表现出色，但容易受到节点属性和图结构本身带来的偏见影响，图神经网络公平性研究成为关键挑战。

Method: 采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段编辑图结构，提高类别标签的同质性比率同时降低敏感属性标签的同质性比率；第二阶段将改进的监督对比损失和环境损失集成到优化过程中，联合优化预测性能和公平性。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上的实验表明，该模型在分类准确性和公平性指标方面均优于CAF及多个最先进的图学习方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效缓解图神经网络中的偏见问题，在保持高预测性能的同时显著提升模型公平性。

Abstract: In recent years, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have achieved remarkable success in tasks such as node classification, link prediction, and graph representation learning. However, they remain susceptible to biases that can arise not only from node attributes but also from the graph structure itself. Addressing fairness in GNNs has therefore emerged as a critical research challenge. In this work, we propose a novel model for training fairness-aware GNNs by improving the counterfactual augmented fair graph neural network framework (CAF). Specifically, our approach introduces a two-phase training strategy: in the first phase, we edit the graph to increase homophily ratio with respect to class labels while reducing homophily ratio with respect to sensitive attribute labels; in the second phase, we integrate a modified supervised contrastive loss and environmental loss into the optimization process, enabling the model to jointly improve predictive performance and fairness. Experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that our model outperforms CAF and several state-of-the-art graph-based learning methods in both classification accuracy and fairness metrics.

</details>


### [29] [Communication-free Sampling and 4D Hybrid Parallelism for Scalable Mini-batch GNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02651)
*Cunyang Wei,Siddharth Singh,Aishwarya Sarkar,Daniel Nichols,Tisha Patel,Aditya K. Ranjan,Sayan Ghosh,Ali Jannesari,Nathan R. Tallent,Abhinav Bhatele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ScaleGNN，一个结合无通信分布式采样、3D并行矩阵乘法和数据并行的4D并行框架，在2048 GPU规模上实现3.5倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式mini-batch GNN训练方法存在两大瓶颈：(1)昂贵的采样方法导致性能开销大；(2)基于数据并行的方法在扩展到大量GPU时扩展性有限。

Method: 提出ScaleGNN 4D并行框架：(1)设计均匀顶点采样算法实现无通信的分布式采样，每个进程独立构建本地mini-batch；(2)采用3D并行矩阵乘法(PMM)降低通信开销并支持更大规模GPU扩展；(3)额外优化包括采样与训练重叠、低精度数据传输、核融合以及通信-计算重叠。

Result: 在Perlmutter(2048 GPU)、Frontier(2048 GCD)和Tuolumne(1024 GPU)上实现强扩展性；在Perlmutter的ogbn-products数据集上相比SOTA基线实现3.5倍端到端训练加速。

Conclusion: ScaleGNN通过创新的4D并行设计有效解决了分布式GNN训练的可扩展性问题，在多个大规模超级计算机上验证了其优越的扩展性能和训练效率。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are widely used for learning on graph datasets derived from various real-world scenarios. Learning from extremely large graphs requires distributed training, and mini-batching with sampling is a popular approach for parallelizing GNN training. Existing distributed mini-batch approaches have significant performance bottlenecks due to expensive sampling methods and limited scaling when using data parallelism. In this work, we present ScaleGNN, a 4D parallel framework for scalable mini-batch GNN training that combines communication-free distributed sampling, 3D parallel matrix multiplication (PMM), and data parallelism. ScaleGNN introduces a uniform vertex sampling algorithm, enabling each process (GPU device) to construct its local mini-batch, i.e., subgraph partitions without any inter-process communication. 3D PMM enables scaling mini-batch training to much larger GPU counts than vanilla data parallelism with significantly lower communication overheads. We also present additional optimizations to overlap sampling with training, reduce communication overhead by sending data in lower precision, kernel fusion, and communication-computation overlap. We evaluate ScaleGNN on five graph datasets and demonstrate strong scaling up to 2048 GPUs on Perlmutter, 2048 GCDs on Frontier, and 1024 GPUs on Tuolumne. On Perlmutter, ScaleGNN achieves 3.5x end-to-end training speedup over the SOTA baseline on ogbn-products.

</details>


### [30] [Haiku to Opus in Just 10 bits: LLMs Unlock Massive Compression Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02343)
*Roy Rinberg,Annabelle Michael Carrell,Simon Henniger,Nicholas Carlini,Keri Warr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型生成文本的压缩方法，发现在无损压缩中利用LoRA适配器可将压缩效果提升2倍；在有损压缩中，通过提示模型重写再编码可实现约0.03的压缩比。研究还提出了“提问压缩”（QA）交互协议，通过小模型向大模型提问二值问题来高效传递信息，在多个基准测试上实现了0.0006至0.004的压缩比，比现有LLM压缩方法高出100倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM文本压缩研究主要关注传输完整响应，压缩效率有限。本研究旨在探索无损和有损两种压缩机制下的压缩-计算权衡边界，并设计更高效的交互式协议来传递知识，突破传统单向压缩的性能瓶颈。

Method: 1）无损压缩：将领域自适应的LoRA适配器集成到LLM算术编码框架中；
2）有损压缩：先提示模型对响应进行简洁重写，再进行算术编码；
3）交互式有损协议（QA）：受“二十个问题”游戏启发，小模型通过向强模型迭代提问yes/no问题来精炼输出，每次回答恰好传递1比特信息。

Result: 在无损压缩场景，LoRA适配器使LLM算术编码性能相比基座模型提升2倍；有损压缩的简洁重写方法达到约0.03的压缩比，较压缩原始响应提升2倍。QA交互协议在8个数学、科学、代码基准上，仅用10个二值问题即可恢复小模型与大模型之间23%至72%的能力差距（标准基准）和7%至38%（困难基准），压缩比达0.0006至0.004，比先前的LLM压缩方法高出100倍以上。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了压缩-计算前沿：更多计算可以换取更高压缩率。交互式协议能以极低比特成本高效传递知识，大幅优于传输完整响应的传统方法。这为LLM通信、蒸馏和高效部署提供了新的方向，表明交互式知识传递是一种极具潜力的范式。

Abstract: We study the compression of LLM-generated text across lossless and lossy regimes, characterizing a compression-compute frontier where more compression is possible at the cost of more compute. For lossless compression, domain-adapted LoRA adapters can improve LLM-based arithmetic coding by 2x over compression with the base LLM alone. For lossy compression, prompting a model for a succinct rewrite then applying arithmetic coding can achieve compression ratios of approximately 0.03, a 2x improvement over compressing the original response.
  We further introduce Question-Asking compression (QA), an interactive lossy protocol inspired by the game 'Twenty Questions'. A small model iteratively refines its response by asking yes/no questions to a stronger model, transferring exactly one bit per answer. On 8 benchmarks spanning math, science, and code, 10 binary questions recover 23% to 72% of the capability gap between a small and large model on standard benchmarks and 7% to 38% on harder benchmarks, achieving compression ratios of 0.0006 to 0.004. This is over 100x smaller than prior LLM-based compression (Deletang et al., 2024), suggesting that interactive protocols can transfer knowledge far more efficiently than transmitting full responses.

</details>


### [31] [FedSQ: Optimized Weight Averaging via Fixed Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02990)
*Cristian Pérez-Corral,Jose I. Mestre,Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Manuel F. Dolz,José Duato,Enrique S. Quintana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedSQ方法，通过冻结预训练模型的结构知识（ReLU门控模式）而仅优化和聚合量化知识，解决了联邦学习中的数据异构性和客户端漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临数据非独立同分布（non-i.i.d.）导致的统计异质性问题，以及简单权重平均在客户端漂移下的不稳定性。现有跨机构部署常从强预训练模型热启动，近期研究表明ReLU类门控机制（结构知识）比参数值（量化知识）更早稳定。

Method: 提出FedSQ（联邦结构-量化学习）方法，基于深度网络的双副本分段线性视角。该方法冻结预训练模型的结构副本以诱导固定的二元门控掩码，仅对量化副本进行本地优化和跨轮聚合。固定门控将学习限定为机制内的仿射修正，从而在异构分区下稳定聚合过程。

Result: 在两个卷积神经网络骨干上的实验表明，FedSQ在i.i.d.和Dirichlet数据划分下均提升了鲁棒性，相比标准基线可减少达到最佳验证性能所需的轮次，同时在迁移学习场景中保持了准确率。

Conclusion: FedSQ通过分离并固定结构知识、仅优化量化知识的策略，有效缓解了联邦学习中数据异构性带来的挑战，为迁移初始化的联邦学习场景提供了一种稳定高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative training across organizations without sharing raw data, but it is hindered by statistical heterogeneity (non-i.i.d.\ client data) and by instability of naive weight averaging under client drift. In many cross-silo deployments, FL is warm-started from a strong pretrained backbone (e.g., ImageNet-1K) and then adapted to local domains. Motivated by recent evidence that ReLU-like gating regimes (structural knowledge) stabilize earlier than the remaining parameter values (quantitative knowledge), we propose FedSQ (Federated Structural-Quantitative learning), a transfer-initialized neural federated procedure based on a DualCopy, piecewise-linear view of deep networks. FedSQ freezes a structural copy of the pretrained model to induce fixed binary gating masks during federated fine-tuning, while only a quantitative copy is optimized locally and aggregated across rounds. Fixing the gating reduces learning to within-regime affine refinements, which stabilizes aggregation under heterogeneous partitions. Experiments on two convolutional neural network backbones under i.i.d.\ and Dirichlet splits show that FedSQ improves robustness and can reduce rounds-to-best validation performance relative to standard baselines while preserving accuracy in the transfer setting.

</details>


### [32] [UI-Oceanus: Scaling GUI Agents with Synthetic Environmental Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02345)
*Mengzhou Wu,Yuzhe Guo,Yuan Cao,Haochuan Lu,Songhe Zhu,Pingzhe Qu,Xin Chen,Kang Qin,Zhongpu Wang,Xiaode Zhang,Xinyi Wang,Wei Dai,Gang Cao,Yuetang Deng,Zhi Gong,Dezhi Ran,Linyi Li,Wei Yang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UI-Oceanus框架，通过前向动力学预测（生成式预测未来界面状态）利用低成本自主探索数据训练GUI智能体，在在线导航任务上相比基线提升16.8%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用GUI智能体的规模化面临两大瓶颈：人工演示数据成本高昂导致数据可扩展性受限，以及合成教师监督存在“蒸馏天花板”效应。现有方法过分依赖模仿高层轨迹，缺乏对交互本质的理解。

Method: 提出UI-Oceanus框架，将学习重心从模仿轨迹转向通过环境真实反馈掌握交互物理规律。系统性地研究自监督目标，发现前向动力学（生成式预测未来界面状态）是可扩展性的主要驱动因素，其重要性显著超过逆向推理。该框架将低成本自主探索（由系统执行直接验证）转化为高密度生成式监督信号，构建稳健的内部世界模型，并采用持续预训练（CPT）策略。

Result: 在离线基准测试中，使用合成动力学数据进行持续预训练的模型相比非CPT基线平均成功率提升7%；在真实世界在线导航任务中，该优势放大至16.8%的提升。同时观察到导航性能随合成数据量增加而扩展。

Conclusion: 以前向预测建模为基础训练智能体，为可扩展的GUI自动化提供了优越路径，能够实现稳健的跨域适应性和组合泛化能力，有效突破了人工演示和知识蒸馏的局限性。

Abstract: Scaling generalist GUI agents is hindered by the data scalability bottleneck of expensive human demonstrations and the "distillation ceiling" of synthetic teacher supervision. To transcend these limitations, we propose UI-Oceanus, a framework that shifts the learning focus from mimicking high-level trajectories to mastering interaction physics via ground-truth environmental feedback. Through a systematic investigation of self-supervised objectives, we identify that forward dynamics, defined as the generative prediction of future interface states, acts as the primary driver for scalability and significantly outweighs inverse inference. UI-Oceanus leverages this insight by converting low-cost autonomous exploration, which is verified directly by system execution, into high-density generative supervision to construct a robust internal world model. Experimental evaluations across a series of models demonstrate the decisive superiority of our approach: models utilizing Continual Pre-Training (CPT) on synthetic dynamics outperform non-CPT baselines with an average success rate improvement of 7% on offline benchmarks, which amplifies to a 16.8% gain in real-world online navigation. Furthermore, we observe that navigation performance scales with synthetic data volume. These results confirm that grounding agents in forward predictive modeling offers a superior pathway to scalable GUI automation with robust cross-domain adaptability and compositional generalization.

</details>


### [33] [DrugPlayGround: Benchmarking Large Language Models and Embeddings for Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02346)
*Tianyu Liu,Sihan Jiang,Fan Zhang,Kunyang Sun,Teresa Head-Gordon,Hongyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了DrugPlayGround框架，用于评估和基准测试大语言模型（LLMs）在药物发现任务中的性能，包括生药物物理化学特征、药物协同作用、药物-蛋白质相互作用及生理反应的文本描述，旨在客观评估LLM相对于传统药物发现平台的优势与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在药物发现研究中迅速崛起，能够加速假设生成、优化候选药物优先级并提高药物发现流程的可扩展性和成本效益。然而，目前缺乏对LLM性能的客观评估，难以确定其相对于传统药物发现平台的优势和局限性。

Method: 开发了DrugPlayGround框架，用于评估和基准测试LLM在生成药物物理化学特征、药物协同作用、药物-蛋白质相互作用以及药物分子引入的生理反应的文本描述方面的性能。该框架还与领域专家合作，提供详细解释以验证LLM的预测，从而测试LLM的化学和生物推理能力。

Result: DrugPlayGround框架能够系统地评估LLM在药物发现各阶段的表现，通过领域专家的参与验证LLM的预测，测试其化学和生物推理能力，为LLM在药物发现前沿的更大应用提供支持。

Conclusion: DrugPlayGround为客观评估LLM在药物发现中的性能提供了有效工具，通过测试LLM的推理能力和提供详细解释，推动了LLM在药物发现各阶段的更广泛应用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are in the ascendancy for research in drug discovery, offering unprecedented opportunities to reshape drug research by accelerating hypothesis generation, optimizing candidate prioritization, and enabling more scalable and cost-effective drug discovery pipelines. However there is currently a lack of objective assessments of LLM performance to ascertain their advantages and limitations over traditional drug discovery platforms. To tackle this emergent problem, we have developed DrugPlayGround, a framework to evaluate and benchmark LLM performance for generating meaningful text-based descriptions of physiochemical drug characteristics, drug synergism, drug-protein interactions, and the physiological response to perturbations introduced by drug molecules. Moreover, DrugPlayGround is designed to work with domain experts to provide detailed explanations for justifying the predictions of LLMs, thereby testing LLMs for chemical and biological reasoning capabilities to push their greater use at the frontier of drug discovery at all of its stages.

</details>


### [34] [Towards Near-Real-Time Telemetry-Aware Routing with Neural Routing Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02927)
*Andreas Boltres,Niklas Freymuth,Benjamin Schichtholz,Michael König,Gerhard Neumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LOGGIA：一种考虑通信延迟的可扩展图神经网络路由算法，在真实延迟条件下表现优异，且完全本地化部署效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经网络的路由算法要么假设不现实的无延迟全局状态，要么仅使用本地遥测数据，忽略了网络信息聚合过程中的通信延迟，导致其在真实环境中的可部署性不明确。

Method: 将遥感感知路由建模为延迟感知的闭环控制问题，提出LOGGIA算法。该算法基于拓扑和遥测图预测对数空间链路权重，采用数据驱动预训练和在线强化学习两阶段训练。

Result: 在合成和真实网络拓扑及混合TCP/UDP流量序列测试中，LOGGIA一致优于最短路径基准；而在引入真实延迟后，现有神经基准算法失效。实验还发现神经网络路由算法在完全本地化部署时性能最佳。

Conclusion: 在路由算法设计中显式建模通信和推理延迟至关重要，本地化部署策略优于集中式决策，LOGGIA为实用化神经网络路由提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Routing algorithms are crucial for efficient computer network operations, and in many settings they must be able to react to traffic bursts within milliseconds. Live telemetry data can provide informative signals to routing algorithms, and recent work has trained neural networks to exploit such signals for traffic-aware routing. Yet, aggregating network-wide information is subject to communication delays, and existing neural approaches either assume unrealistic delay-free global states, or restrict routers to purely local telemetry. This leaves their deployability in real-world environments unclear. We cast telemetry-aware routing as a delay-aware closed-loop control problem and introduce a framework that trains and evaluates neural routing algorithms, while explicitly modeling communication and inference delays. On top of this framework, we propose LOGGIA, a scalable graph neural routing algorithm that predicts log-space link weights from attributed topology-and-telemetry graphs. It utilizes a data-driven pre-training stage, followed by on-policy Reinforcement Learning. Across synthetic and real network topologies, and unseen mixed TCP/UDP traffic sequences, LOGGIA consistently outperforms shortest-path baselines, whereas neural baselines fail once realistic delays are enforced. Our experiments further suggest that neural routing algorithms like LOGGIA perform best when deployed fully locally, i.e., observing network states and inferring actions at every router individually, as opposed to centralized decision making.

</details>


### [35] [FTimeXer: Frequency-aware Time-series Transformer with Exogenous variables for Robust Carbon Footprint Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02347)
*Qingzhong Li,Yue Hu,Zhou Long,Qingchang Ma,Hui Ma,Jinhai Sa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FTimeXer，一种频率感知的时间序列Transformer模型，通过FFT驱动的频率分支和随机外生变量掩码策略，有效解决了电网碳足迹预测中的非平稳性、周期性模式捕捉以及不规则外生输入问题，在三个真实数据集上取得了优于基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网碳强度的准确预测对于产品碳足迹（PCF）核算和脱碳决策至关重要，然而现有方法在面对碳强度的高非平稳性时，难以有效利用周期性和振荡模式，且在处理不规则外生输入（如数据缺失或错位）时性能较差。

Method: 提出FTimeXer模型，包含两个核心创新：1）设计FFT驱动的频率分支并结合门控时频融合机制，以有效捕捉多尺度周期性；2）采用随机外生变量掩码结合一致性正则化训练方案，以减少虚假相关性并增强模型对不规则输入的稳定性。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，该方法相较于强基线模型取得了持续的性能改进，能够提供更可靠的电网碳因子预测结果。

Conclusion: FTimeXer通过频率感知设计和鲁棒的训练策略，成功解决了电网碳足迹预测中的关键挑战，为有效的PCF核算和明智的脱碳决策提供了可靠的技术支持。

Abstract: Accurate and up-to-date forecasting of the power grid's carbon footprint is crucial for effective product carbon footprint (PCF) accounting and informed decarbonization decisions. However, the carbon intensity of the grid exhibits high non-stationarity, and existing methods often struggle to effectively leverage periodic and oscillatory patterns. Furthermore, these methods tend to perform poorly when confronted with irregular exogenous inputs, such as missing data or misalignment. To tackle these challenges, we propose FTimeXer, a frequency-aware time-series Transformer designed with a robust training scheme that accommodates exogenous factors. FTimeXer features an Fast Fourier Transform (FFT)-driven frequency branch combined with gated time-frequency fusion, allowing it to capture multi-scale periodicity effectively. It also employs stochastic exogenous masking in conjunction with consistency regularization, which helps reduce spurious correlations and enhance stability. Experiments conducted on three real-world datasets show consistent improvements over strong baselines. As a result, these enhancements lead to more reliable forecasts of grid carbon factors, which are essential for effective PCF accounting and informed decision-making regarding decarbonization.

</details>


### [36] [Contextual Intelligence The Next Leap for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02348)
*André Biedenkapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的上下文分类法（异源性和自源性上下文），并指出了实现上下文智能的三个关键研究方向，旨在提高强化学习智能体的泛化能力和现实世界部署效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管强化学习在多个领域取得了显著成功，但学习到的策略往往无法在训练分布之外进行泛化，限制了其在现实世界中的影响。现有的上下文强化学习（cRL）将上下文视为单一的静态可观察变量，这种方法制约了智能体的泛化能力。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的上下文分类法，将上下文分为异源性（环境强加的）和自源性（智能体驱动的）因素。基于此分类，确定了三个关键研究方向：（1）利用异构上下文学习，使智能体能够推理其对世界的影响及反之亦然；（2）多时间尺度建模，以处理异源性变量的缓慢变化与自源性变量的快速变化；（3）整合抽象的高级上下文，如角色、资源、监管机制和非物理描述符。

Result: 构建了一个全面的上下文分类体系和研究框架，将上下文确立为第一类建模原语，使智能体能够推理自身属性、世界约束以及它们随时间的演变关系。

Conclusion: 通过将上下文作为核心建模原语并解决三个关键研究方向，可以催生新一代具有上下文感知能力的智能体，使其能够在现实世界中安全高效地部署。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has produced spectacular results in games, robotics, and continuous control. Yet, despite these successes, learned policies often fail to generalize beyond their training distribution, limiting real-world impact. Recent work on contextual RL (cRL) shows that exposing agents to environment characteristics -- contexts -- can improve zero-shot transfer. So far, the community has treated context as a monolithic, static observable, an approach that constrains the generalization capabilities of RL agents.
  To achieve contextual intelligence we first propose a novel taxonomy of contexts that separates allogenic (environment-imposed) from autogenic (agent-driven) factors. We identify three fundamental research directions that must be addressed to promote truly contextual intelligence: (1) Learning with heterogeneous contexts to explicitly exploit the taxonomy levels so agents can reason about their influence on the world and vice versa; (2) Multi-time-scale modeling to recognize that allogenic variables evolve slowly or remain static, whereas autogenic variables may change within an episode, potentially requiring different learning mechanisms; (3) Integration of abstract, high-level contexts to incorporate roles, resource & regulatory regimes, uncertainties, and other non-physical descriptors that crucially influence behavior.
  We envision context as a first-class modeling primitive, empowering agents to reason about who they are, what the world permits, and how both evolve over time. By doing so, we aim to catalyze a new generation of context-aware agents that can be deployed safely and efficiently in the real world.

</details>


### [37] [OPRIDE: Offline Preference-based Reinforcement Learning via In-Dataset Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02349)
*Yiqin Yang,Hao Hu,Yihuan Mao,Jin Zhang,Chengjie Wu,Yuhua Jiang,Xu Yang,Runpeng Xie,Yi Fan,Bo Liu,Yang Gao,Bo Xu,Chongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了OPRIDE算法，通过最大化查询信息量的探索策略和缓解奖励函数过优化的折扣调度机制，显著提升了离线偏好强化学习的查询效率，在更少查询下实现了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好强化学习（PbRL）能避免复杂的奖励设计并与人类意图更好对齐，但获取人类偏好反馈成本高昂且耗时，离线PbRL中查询效率低下的问题尤为突出，主要原因是探索效率不足和学习奖励函数的过优化。

Method: 提出OPRIDE算法，包含两个核心组件：(1) 原则性探索策略，通过最大化查询的信息量来提升探索效率；(2) 折扣调度机制，用于缓解学习奖励函数的过优化问题。

Result: 实验表明OPRIDE显著优于先前方法，以明显更少的查询次数实现了强劲性能；提供了算法效率的理论保证；在多种运动、操作和导航任务中验证了方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: OPRIDE有效解决了离线偏好强化学习中查询效率低的问题，在理论和实践上均证明了其优越性，为减少人类反馈需求、推动PbRL实际应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Preference-based reinforcement learning (PbRL) can help avoid sophisticated reward designs and align better with human intentions, showing great promise in various real-world applications.
